   492	    const p = bundlePaths(cwd);
   493	    nodeFs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true });
   494	    const foreignOwner = JSON.stringify({ host: 'another-machine', pid: 5, startTime: 1, fencingToken: 'X', acquiredAt: NOW, heartbeatAt: NOW });
   495	    nodeFs.writeFileSync(`${p.lockDir}/owner.json`, foreignOwner);
   496	    const before = snapshotTree(cwd);
   497	
   498	    const ck = makeRealIo(cwd, { stdin: evt({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'under-cross-host-lock' } }, 'xhost-1', { sessionHint: 'orig-sess' }) });
   499	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], ck);
   500	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'a cross-host refusal is still hook-safe (exit 0)');
   501	    assert.match(ck.stderrText(), /host/i, 'the warning names the cross-host cause');
   502	    assert.deepEqual(snapshotTree(cwd), before, 'a cross-host refusal mutates nothing');
   503	
   504	    const beforeRecover = snapshotTree(cwd);
   505	    const r = recoverLock(cwd, makeRealIo(cwd, { processAlive: () => false }), { force: true });
   506	    assert.equal(r.recovered, false, 'a cross-host lock is never provably safe to steal, even with force (real fs)');
   507	    assert.equal(nodeFs.readFileSync(`${p.lockDir}/owner.json`, 'utf8'), foreignOwner, 'the cross-host owner.json is byte-for-byte intact');
   508	    assert.deepEqual(snapshotTree(cwd), beforeRecover, '(iter-2) the refused forced recovery mutates nothing anywhere under .handoff/');
   509	  });
   510	
   511	  it('(F2) live same-host owner: recoverLock --force is refused, told to terminate the process, lock intact', async () => {
   512	    const cwd = writeBundleRepo('baton-e2e-liveforce-', 'claude-code', 'open');
   513	    const p = bundlePaths(cwd);
   514	    nodeFs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true });
   515	    const liveOwner = JSON.stringify({ host: 'e2e-host', pid: 777, startTime: 42, fencingToken: 'L', acquiredAt: NOW, heartbeatAt: NOW });
   516	    nodeFs.writeFileSync(`${p.lockDir}/owner.json`, liveOwner);
   517	
   518	    const beforeRecover = snapshotTree(cwd);
   519	    const io = makeRealIo(cwd, { processAlive: (pid) => pid === 777 || pid === 4242 });
   520	    const r = recoverLock(cwd, io, { force: true });
   521	    assert.equal(r.recovered, false, 'force NEVER recovers a live same-host owner');
   522	    assert.match(r.refusedReason, /terminate|live|alive|running|777/i, 'the refusal says to terminate the live process');
   523	    assert.equal(nodeFs.readFileSync(`${p.lockDir}/owner.json`, 'utf8'), liveOwner, 'the live owner lock is byte-for-byte intact');
   524	    assert.deepEqual(snapshotTree(cwd), beforeRecover, '(iter-2) the refused forced recovery mutates nothing anywhere under .handoff/');
   525	  });
   526	
   527	  it('(F2, staged per E6) pause-after-final-fence-check: a holder that paused past its last check fast-aborts, the write never lands', async () => {
   528	    const cwd = writeBundleRepo('baton-e2e-fence-', 'claude-code', 'open');
   529	    const p = bundlePaths(cwd);
   530	    const io = makeRealIo(cwd);
   531	    const guardedTarget = `${p.dir}/fence-guarded-write.json`;
   532	    let fenceError = null;
   533	    let guardedRan = false;
   534	
   535	    withLock(cwd, io, (token) => {
   536	      // First guarded write while current: proceeds.
   537	      const ok = guardedWrite(cwd, io, token, () => 'went-through');
   538	      assert.equal(ok, 'went-through');
   539	
   540	      // The competing takeover: owner.json is republished with a new token on the
   541	      // REAL disk while this holder is paused past its final token check.
   542	      const owner = JSON.parse(nodeFs.readFileSync(`${p.lockDir}/owner.json`, 'utf8'));
   543	      nodeFs.writeFileSync(`${p.lockDir}/owner.json`, JSON.stringify({ ...owner, fencingToken: 'STOLEN-BY-COMPETITOR' }));
   544	
   545	      // The paused holder resumes and attempts its write: must fast-abort.
   546	      try {
   547	        guardedWrite(cwd, io, token, () => {
   548	          guardedRan = true;
   549	          nodeFs.writeFileSync(guardedTarget, '{"clobbered":true}');
   550	        });
   551	      } catch (e) {
   552	        fenceError = e;
   553	      }
   554	    });
   555	
   556	    assert.ok(fenceError, 'the stale holder aborts instead of clobbering');
   557	    assert.equal(fenceError.name, 'FencingError', 'the abort is the fencing layer');
   558	    assert.equal(guardedRan, false, 'the guarded fn never ran after the fence check failed');
   559	    assert.equal(nodeFs.existsSync(guardedTarget), false, 'the stale write never landed on the real fs');
   560	
   561	    // (iter-2) The clobber check extends to RELEASE: after withLock returns, the
   562	    // stale holder's finally-path must not have deleted or rewritten the
   563	    // competitor's lock — release is fence-guarded, like every other write.
   564	    assert.ok(nodeFs.existsSync(`${p.lockDir}/owner.json`), "the competitor's lock survives the stale holder's release path");
   565	    assert.equal(
   566	      JSON.parse(nodeFs.readFileSync(`${p.lockDir}/owner.json`, 'utf8')).fencingToken,
   567	      'STOLEN-BY-COMPETITOR',
   568	      "the competitor's fencing token is untouched after the stale holder exits",
   569	    );
   570	    nodeFs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); // test-owned cleanup of the staged competitor lock
   571	  });
   572	
   573	  it('(F2) prepare-vs-checkpoint race: an intervening checkpoint stales the token; the commit is rejected and mutates nothing', async () => {
   574	    const cwd = writeBundleRepo('baton-e2e-race-', 'claude-code', 'sealed');
   575	
   576	    const prep = makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW });
   577	    assert.equal(await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--prepare', '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], prep), 0);
   578	    const { token } = JSON.parse(prep.stdoutText()).data;
   579	
   580	    // The race: the origin session lands one more checkpoint after prepare.
   581	    const ck = makeRealIo(cwd, { stdin: evt({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'intervening-turn' } }, 'race-1', { sessionHint: 'orig-sess' }) });
   582	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], ck), 0);
   583	    const afterCheckpoint = snapshotTree(cwd);
   584	
   585	    const commitIo = makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW });
   586	    const code = await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--commit', token, '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], commitIo);
   587	    assert.equal(code, 1, 'the raced token is rejected (bundle revision drifted)');
   588	    assert.match(commitIo.stdoutText(), /re-?prepare|stale|drift/i, 'the rejection names re-prepare');
   589	    assert.deepEqual(snapshotTree(cwd), afterCheckpoint, 'the rejected commit leaves the entire .handoff/ tree byte-identical');
   590	    assert.equal(loadBundle(cwd, makeRealIo(cwd)).bundle.generation, 1, 'no generation opened');
   591	  });
   592	
   593	  it('(F2) foreign-session rejection: a stable foreign hint is refused (exit 0, tree untouched, --take-over named)', async () => {
   594	    const cwd = writeBundleRepo('baton-e2e-foreign-', 'claude-code', 'open', {
   595	      decisions: [{ seq: 1, ts: NOW, summary: 'belongs-to-orig' }],
   596	      journalSeq: 1,
   597	    });
   598	    const before = snapshotTree(cwd);
   599	
   600	    const io = makeRealIo(cwd, { stdin: evt({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'from-intruder' } }, 'foreign-1', { sessionHint: 'intruder-sess' }) });
   601	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io);
   602	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'foreign-session rejection is still exit 0 (hook safety)');
   603	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /--take-over/, 'the warning names the --take-over escape hatch');
   604	    assert.deepEqual(snapshotTree(cwd), before, 'the rejected foreign event leaves the real tree byte-identical');
   605	  });
   606	
   607	  it('(F2) --take-over: archives the active bundle and starts fresh, owned by the incoming session', async () => {
   608	    const cwd = writeBundleRepo('baton-e2e-takeover-', 'claude-code', 'open', {
   609	      decisions: [{ seq: 1, ts: NOW, summary: 'OLD-DECISION' }],
   610	      journalSeq: 1,
   611	    });
   612	
   613	    const io = makeRealIo(cwd, { stdin: evt({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'from-intruder' } }, 'takeover-1', { sessionHint: 'intruder-sess' }) });
   614	    const code = await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'codex', '--take-over'], io);
   615	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   616	
   617	    const history = nodeFs.readdirSync(bundlePaths(cwd).historyDir);
   618	    assert.ok(history.some((n) => /\.takeover\.json$/.test(n)), 'the prior bundle is archived as a takeover rotation on the real fs');
   619	
   620	    const fresh = loadBundle(cwd, makeRealIo(cwd)).bundle;
   621	    assert.equal(fresh.origin.sessionHint, 'intruder-sess', 'the fresh bundle is owned by the taking-over session');
   622	    assert.equal(fresh.origin.platform, 'codex', 'the fresh bundle adopts the taking-over platform');
   623	    assert.ok(fresh.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'from-intruder'), 'the new event applies to the fresh bundle');
   624	    assert.ok(!fresh.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'OLD-DECISION'), 'the fresh bundle does not inherit the archived session state');
   625	  });
   626	
   627	  it('(F2) double-commit: the second commit of an already-spent token fails cleanly, no second archive or generation', async () => {
   628	    const cwd = writeBundleRepo('baton-e2e-double-', 'claude-code', 'sealed');
   629	
   630	    const prep = makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW });
   631	    assert.equal(await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--prepare', '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], prep), 0);
   632	    const { token } = JSON.parse(prep.stdoutText()).data;
   633	
   634	    assert.equal(await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--commit', token, '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW })), 0, 'the first commit succeeds');
   635	    const afterFirst = snapshotTree(cwd);
   636	
   637	    const io2 = makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW });
   638	    const code2 = await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--commit', token, '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], io2);
   639	    assert.equal(code2, 1, 'a double-commit of the same token fails cleanly');
   640	    assert.match(io2.stdoutText(), /re-?prepare|stale|drift/i, 'the failure names re-prepare');
   641	    assert.deepEqual(snapshotTree(cwd), afterFirst, 'the failed double-commit changes nothing');
   642	    assert.equal(receiveFreezes(cwd).length, 1, 'no second receive archive is created');
   643	    assert.equal(loadBundle(cwd, makeRealIo(cwd)).bundle.generation, 2, 'the generation did not advance again');
   644	  });
   645	
   646	  it('competing receivers: the first commit wins, the second (now-stale) token is rejected requiring re-prepare', async () => {
   647	    const cwd = writeBundleRepo('baton-e2e-compete-', 'claude-code', 'sealed');
   648	
   649	    const prep1 = makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW });
   650	    assert.equal(await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--prepare', '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], prep1), 0);
   651	    const t1 = JSON.parse(prep1.stdoutText()).data.token;
   652	    const prep2 = makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW });
   653	    assert.equal(await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'cursor', '--prepare', '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], prep2), 0);
   654	    const t2 = JSON.parse(prep2.stdoutText()).data.token;
   655	
   656	    assert.equal(await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--commit', t1, '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW })), 0, 'the first receiver commits');
   657	
   658	    const io2 = makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW });
   659	    const code2 = await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'cursor', '--commit', t2, '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], io2);
   660	    assert.equal(code2, 1, 'the competing receiver commit is rejected — the bundle revision moved under it');
   661	    assert.match(io2.stdoutText(), /re-?prepare|stale|drift/i, 'the rejection tells the loser to re-prepare');
   662	    assert.equal(loadBundle(cwd, makeRealIo(cwd)).bundle.origin.platform, 'codex', 'the winner keeps ownership');
   663	  });
   664	
   665	  it('prepare-without-commit: prepare mutates nothing and leaves the bundle sealed + receivable', async () => {
   666	    const cwd = writeBundleRepo('baton-e2e-prep-', 'claude-code', 'sealed');
   667	    const before = snapshotTree(cwd);
   668	
   669	    const io = makeRealIo(cwd, { now: NOW });
   670	    assert.equal(await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--prepare', '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', CC_LIMIT, '--json'], io), 0);
   671	
   672	    assert.deepEqual(snapshotTree(cwd), before, 'prepare is read-only — no state changes without commit');
   673	    assert.equal(loadBundle(cwd, makeRealIo(cwd)).bundle.handoff.status, 'sealed', 'the bundle is untouched and still receivable');
   674	  });
   675	});
     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     4	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     5	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     6	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
     7	import { cmdCheckpoint } from '../../core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs';
     8	import { cmdReceive } from '../../core/src/commands/receive.mjs';
     9	import { bundlePaths, loadBundle } from '../../core/src/bundle/store.mjs';
    10	
    11	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12	// Gate-2 fix 2 (reviewer-a finding 2 / reviewer-b findings 3+5): session
    13	// ownership seams. An unverified owner — a fresh auto-seed (null hint) or a
    14	// receive that defaulted to a host-derived hint — must ADOPT the first stable
    15	// same-platform session, after which semantic isolation engages. The receiving
    16	// session's next real hook checkpoint (carrying a hint the receive never saw)
    17	// must be accepted as first-party, never rejected as foreign.
    18	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19	
    20	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    21	const repoRoot = join(here, '..', '..');
    22	const SIG_PATH = join(repoRoot, 'core', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    23	const SIG_CONTENT = readFileSync(SIG_PATH, 'utf8');
    24	
    25	const ROOT = '/repo';
    26	const paths = bundlePaths(ROOT);
    27	const NOW = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z';
    28	
    29	const CONFIG_TEXT = JSON.stringify({
    30	  schema: 'baton/config@1',
    31	  roles: { implementer: ['claude-code/claude-fable-5', 'codex/gpt-5.6-sol@xhigh'] },
    32	  platforms: { 'claude-code': {}, codex: {}, cursor: {} },
    33	  defaults: { 'claude-code': 'claude-fable-5', codex: 'gpt-5.6-sol', cursor: 'composer' },
    34	});
    35	
    36	const GIT_CLEAN = {
    37	  'git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD': { stdout: 'main\n' },
    38	  'git rev-parse HEAD': { stdout: 'abc123\n' },
    39	  'git status --porcelain': { stdout: '' },
    40	  'git diff --cached': { stdout: '' },
    41	  'git diff': { stdout: '' },
    42	  'git ls-files --others --exclude-standard': { stdout: '' },
    43	};
    44	
    45	const event = (obj) => JSON.stringify({ schema: 'baton/event@1', ...obj });
    46	
    47	function sealedBundle() {
    48	  return {
    49	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
    50	    bundleId: 'b_adopt00000000',
    51	    generation: 1,
    52	    createdAt: NOW,
    53	    updatedAt: NOW,
    54	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 'sess-A', unstable: false },
    55	    task: { goal: 'g', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
    56	    plan: { steps: [] },
    57	    decisions: [],
    58	    files: { touched: [] },
    59	    roles: { assignments: {} },
    60	    git: { branch: 'main', headSha: 'abc123', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'd0' },
    61	    handoff: { status: 'sealed', reason: "You've hit your usage limit", reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: 'codex', finalizedAt: NOW, receive_log: [] },
    62	    journalSeq: 0,
    63	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
    64	    dedupeRing: [],
    65	  };
    66	}
    67	
    68	// ===========================================================================
    69	describe('session adoption — auto-seed adopts the first stable session (B5)', () => {
    70	  it('a seed created by a stable-hint event is OWNED by that session; a foreign stable hint is then rejected', async () => {
    71	    const io = makeIo({ stdin: event({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'first' }, sessionHint: 'real-sess-1', unstable: false }) });
    72	
    73	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
    74	    const owned = loadBundle(ROOT, io).bundle;
    75	    assert.equal(owned.origin.sessionHint, 'real-sess-1', 'the seed adopted the first stable session as owner');
    76	    assert.equal(owned.origin.unstable, false);
    77	
    78	    // A different stable session now hits the same bundle: isolation must engage.
    79	    io.stdin = event({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'intruder' }, sessionHint: 'other-sess', unstable: false });
    80	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0, 'rejection is still hook-safe');
    81	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /--take-over/, 'the foreign session is told about --take-over');
    82	    assert.ok(!loadBundle(ROOT, io).bundle.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'intruder'), 'the foreign event did not merge');
    83	  });
    84	});
    85	
    86	// ===========================================================================
    87	describe('session adoption — post-receive first-party checkpoint (B3)', () => {
    88	  it('after a receive with NO --session, a checkpoint carrying a hint receive never saw is ACCEPTED and adopts ownership', async () => {
    89	    const io = makeIo({
    90	      files: {
    91	        [SIG_PATH]: SIG_CONTENT,
    92	        [`${ROOT}/baton.config.json`]: CONFIG_TEXT,
    93	        [paths.snapshot]: JSON.stringify(sealedBundle(), null, 2) + '\n',
    94	        [paths.journal]: '',
    95	      },
    96	      execResults: GIT_CLEAN,
    97	    });
    98	
    99	    // Receive on codex with the DEFAULTED session hint (no --session flag).
   100	    const prep = await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--prepare', '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', 'limit', '--json'], io);
   101	    assert.equal(prep, 0);
   102	    const { token } = JSON.parse(io.stdoutText()).data;
   103	    assert.equal(await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--commit', token, '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', 'limit', '--json'], io), 0);
   104	
   105	    const adopted = loadBundle(ROOT, io).bundle;
   106	    assert.equal(adopted.origin.platform, 'codex');
   107	    assert.equal(adopted.origin.unstable, true, 'a defaulted host-derived hint is marked unstable — not a verified session id');
   108	
   109	    // The real harness session now checkpoints with ITS OWN id — one the
   110	    // receive never saw. This must be first-party, not foreign.
   111	    io.stdin = event({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'post-receive-work' }, sessionHint: 'codex-real-session-xyz', unstable: false, source: 'codex' });
   112	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'codex'], io), 0);
   113	
   114	    const after = loadBundle(ROOT, io).bundle;
   115	    assert.ok(after.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'post-receive-work'), "the receiving session's first real checkpoint is ACCEPTED (plan: subsequent checkpoints are first-party)");
   116	    assert.equal(after.origin.sessionHint, 'codex-real-session-xyz', 'ownership adopted to the real harness session');
   117	    assert.equal(after.origin.unstable, false);
   118	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText(), /--take-over/, 'no foreign-session rejection fired');
   119	  });
   120	
   121	  it('an explicit --session keeps a STABLE owner that later foreign hints cannot displace', async () => {
   122	    const io = makeIo({
   123	      files: {
   124	        [SIG_PATH]: SIG_CONTENT,
   125	        [`${ROOT}/baton.config.json`]: CONFIG_TEXT,
   126	        [paths.snapshot]: JSON.stringify(sealedBundle(), null, 2) + '\n',
   127	        [paths.journal]: '',
   128	      },
   129	      execResults: GIT_CLEAN,
   130	    });
   131	    const prep = await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--prepare', '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', 'limit', '--session', 'codex-known-sess', '--json'], io);
   132	    assert.equal(prep, 0);
   133	    const { token } = JSON.parse(io.stdoutText()).data;
   134	    assert.equal(
   135	      await cmdReceive(['--platform', 'codex', '--commit', token, '--origin', 'claude-code', '--reason', 'limit', '--session', 'codex-known-sess', '--json'], io),
   136	      0,
   137	    );
   138	
   139	    const adopted = loadBundle(ROOT, io).bundle;
   140	    assert.equal(adopted.origin.sessionHint, 'codex-known-sess');
   141	    assert.equal(adopted.origin.unstable, false, 'an explicit session id is a verified stable owner');
   142	
   143	    io.stdin = event({ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 'intruder' }, sessionHint: 'someone-else', unstable: false, source: 'codex' });
   144	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'codex'], io), 0);
   145	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /--take-over/, 'a foreign stable hint against a verified owner is rejected');
   146	    assert.ok(!loadBundle(ROOT, io).bundle.decisions.some((d) => d.summary === 'intruder'));
   147	  });
   148	});
     1	import { describe, it, after } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
     4	import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     5	import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
     6	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     7	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     8	
     9	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	// Gate-2 fix 12 (reviewer-a finding 16): adapter-to-core STATE tests. The
    11	// primary failure case — a Claude Code session dying on rate_limit before it
    12	// can seal — is exercised through the real production chain: the shipped hook
    13	// script marks the bundle limit-hit, and a later Codex/Cursor session start
    14	// (the real `baton session-start` gate) surfaces the pending handoff. Argv
    15	// inspection proves nothing here; every assertion below is persisted state or
    16	// real child-process output.
    17	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18	
    19	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    20	const repoRoot = join(here, '..', '..');
    21	const HOOK = join(repoRoot, 'adapters', 'claude-code', 'scripts', 'hook.mjs');
    22	const BATON = join(repoRoot, 'core', 'bin', 'baton.mjs');
    23	
    24	/** @type {string[]} */
    25	const dirs = [];
    26	after(() => {
    27	  for (const d of dirs) rmSync(d, { recursive: true, force: true });
    28	});
    29	
    30	describe('unsealed limit death → cross-platform session start (full production chain)', () => {
    31	  it('StopFailure(rate_limit) via the hook, then codex session-start surfaces the pending handoff', () => {
    32	    const cwd = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'baton-chain-'));
    33	    dirs.push(cwd);
    34	
    35	    // 1. The dying Claude Code session: hooks.json invokes the hook script.
    36	    const stopFailure = spawnSync('node', [HOOK, 'StopFailure'], {
    37	      cwd,
    38	      input: JSON.stringify({ hook_event_name: 'StopFailure', session_id: 'chain-sess', cwd, error: { type: 'rate_limit' } }),
    39	      encoding: 'utf8',
    40	      env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT: repoRoot },
    41	      timeout: 60_000,
    42	    });
    43	    assert.equal(stopFailure.status, 0, `hook exits 0; stderr: ${stopFailure.stderr}`);

    46	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.status, 'open', 'no seal was written — the degraded shape');
    47	    assert.equal(bundle.handoff.reasonClass, 'usage-limit', 'the limit death is persisted state, not a log line');
    48	
    49	    // 2. The NEXT session on another platform: its SessionStart hook runs the
    50	    // cheap gate and must surface the pending handoff.
    51	    const codexStart = spawnSync('node', [BATON, 'session-start', '--platform', 'codex', '--root', cwd], {
    52	      cwd,
    53	      encoding: 'utf8',
    54	      timeout: 60_000,
    55	    });
    56	    assert.equal(codexStart.status, 0, `session-start always exits 0; stderr: ${codexStart.stderr}`);
    57	    assert.match(codexStart.stdout, /claude-code/, 'the notice names the origin platform');
    58	    assert.match(codexStart.stdout, /limit-hit/, 'the notice names the unsealed limit death');
    59	    assert.match(codexStart.stdout, /receive/, 'the notice points at the receive flow');
    60	
    61	    // 3. Same platform as the origin: quiet — a session's own bundle is not a
    62	    // pending handoff, and the gate never exits nonzero.
    63	    const ownStart = spawnSync('node', [BATON, 'session-start', '--platform', 'claude-code', '--root', cwd], {
    64	      cwd,
    65	      encoding: 'utf8',
    66	      timeout: 60_000,
    67	    });
    68	    assert.equal(ownStart.status, 0);
    69	    assert.equal(ownStart.stdout.trim(), '', 'own-platform bundle stays quiet');
    70	  });

   106	
   107	/** @param {any} s */
   108	function lintSignature(s) {
   109	  if (typeof s.id !== 'string' || s.id.length === 0) throw new Error('signature with missing id');
   110	  if (!PLATFORMS.includes(s.platform)) {
   111	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown platform "${s.platform}" (expected ${PLATFORMS.join('|')})`);
   112	  }
   113	  if (!CLASSES.includes(s.class)) throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown class "${s.class}"`);
   114	  if (!s.matcher || !MATCHER_KINDS.includes(s.matcher.kind)) {
   115	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown matcher kind "${s.matcher?.kind}" (expected ${MATCHER_KINDS.join('|')})`);
   116	  }
   117	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'regex') lintRegex(s.id, s.matcher.pattern);
   118	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'substring' && typeof s.matcher.value !== 'string') {
   119	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: substring matcher needs a string value`);
   120	  }
   121	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'json-field' && typeof s.matcher.path !== 'string') {
   122	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: json-field matcher needs a dotted string path`);
   123	  }
   124	  if (s.resetHint !== undefined) lintRegex(s.id, s.resetHint);
   125	}
   126	
   127	/** @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {{probe?: boolean}} [opts] */
   128	function readTable(fs, path, opts = {}) {
   129	  const raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
   130	  const table = JSON.parse(raw);
   131	  if (table?.schema !== 'baton/signatures@1') {
   132	    throw new Error(`signature table at ${path}: expected schema baton/signatures@1, got ${JSON.stringify(table?.schema)}`);
   133	  }
   134	  if (!Array.isArray(table.signatures)) throw new Error(`signature table at ${path}: signatures must be an array`);
   135	  for (const s of table.signatures) {
   136	    lintSignature(s);
   137	    // Overlay tables are untrusted: the structural lint cannot see group-free
   138	    // pathological shapes (chained stars), so every overlay regex additionally
   139	    // runs against adversarial inputs in a worker with a hard deadline.
   140	    if (opts.probe === true) {
   141	      for (const pattern of [s.matcher?.kind === 'regex' ? s.matcher.pattern : null, s.resetHint ?? null]) {
   142	        if (typeof pattern !== 'string') continue;
   143	        const verdict = probeRegexSafe(pattern, s.matcher?.flags ?? '');
   144	        if (!verdict.safe) throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: ${verdict.reason}`);
   145	      }
   146	    }
   147	  }
   148	  return table;
   149	}
   150	
   151	/**
   152	 * Load + validate the signature table, merging an optional overlay by id
   153	 * (later wins — the drift-absorbing hot-patch surface). Builtin patterns are
   154	 * package-shipped and statically linted; overlay patterns are additionally
   155	 * probe-guarded at load.
   156	 * @param {{builtinPath: string, overlayPath?: string}} paths @param {any} io
   157	 */
   158	export function loadSignatures({ builtinPath, overlayPath }, io) {
   159	  const base = readTable(io.fs, builtinPath);
   160	  if (!overlayPath) return base;
   161	
   162	  const overlay = readTable(io.fs, overlayPath, { probe: true });
   163	  const merged = [...base.signatures];
   164	  for (const s of overlay.signatures) {
   165	    const i = merged.findIndex((b) => b.id === s.id);
   166	    if (i === -1) merged.push(s);
   167	    else merged[i] = s;
   168	  }
   169	  return { ...base, updated: overlay.updated ?? base.updated, signatures: merged };
   170	}

    26	/** loadSignatures against the real builtin plus one overlay regex. */
    27	function loadWithOverlay(pattern) {
    28	  // The builtin table ships with the package — read it through the real fs
    29	  // into the fake so the overlay merge path is exercised end to end.
    30	  const io = makeIo({ files: { '/overlay.json': table(pattern), '/builtin.json': readFileSync(BUILTIN, 'utf8') } });
    31	  return loadSignatures({ builtinPath: '/builtin.json', overlayPath: '/overlay.json' }, io);

     1	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     2	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     3	import { loadSignatures } from '../detect/signatures.mjs';
     4	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
     5	import { emitEnvelope, parseFlags, usageError } from './shared.mjs';
     6	
     7	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
     8	
     9	/** @type {Record<string, number>} */
    10	const EXIT_BY_CLASS = { ok: 0, 'usage-limit': 10, throttle: 11, auth: 12, 'other-error': 13 };
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * `baton detect` — classify harness output / structured error types.
    14	 * Exit codes ARE the verdict (frozen contract): 0 ok · 10 usage-limit ·
    15	 * 11 throttle · 12 auth · 13 other-error · 2 usage error.
    16	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    17	 * @returns {number}
    18	 */
    19	export function cmdDetect(args, io) {
    20	  const { flags } = parseFlags(args);
    21	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
    22	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'detect', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    23	
    24	  let table;
    25	  try {
    26	    table = loadSignatures(
    27	      {
    28	        builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES,
    29	        overlayPath: typeof flags.signatures === 'string' ? flags.signatures : undefined,
    30	      },
    31	      io,
    32	    );
    33	  } catch (err) {
    34	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    35	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-signatures', msg } });
    36	    else io.stderr.write(`baton detect: ${msg}\n`);
    37	    return 1;
    38	  }
    39	
    40	  const text = typeof flags.text === 'string' ? flags.text : typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '';
    41	  const exitCode = flags['exit-code'] !== undefined ? Number(flags['exit-code']) : 0;
    42	
    43	  /** @type {{kind: string, errorType: string} | null} */
    44	  let structured = null;
    45	  if (typeof flags['structured-error-type'] === 'string') {
    46	    structured = {
    47	      kind: platform === 'codex' ? 'exec-json' : 'stop-failure',
    48	      errorType: flags['structured-error-type'],
    49	    };
    50	  }
    51	
    52	  const verdict = classify({ text, exitCode, platform, table, structured });
    53	  const data = { ...verdict, tableVersion: table.schema, tableUpdated: table.updated };
    54	  if (flags.explain) {
    55	    data.tried = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform).map((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.id);
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  if (flags.json) {
    59	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
    60	  } else {
    61	    io.stdout.write(`${verdict.class}${verdict.signatureId ? ` (${verdict.signatureId}, ${verdict.confidence})` : ''}${verdict.resetHint ? ` — resets ${verdict.resetHint}` : ''}\n`);
    62	  }
    63	  return EXIT_BY_CLASS[verdict.class] ?? 13;
    64	}
     1	const PRECEDENCE = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
     2	
     3	/** @type {Record<string, string>} */
     4	const STOP_FAILURE_MAP = {
     5	  rate_limit: 'usage-limit',
     6	  overloaded: 'throttle',
     7	  authentication_failed: 'auth',
     8	  oauth_org_not_allowed: 'auth',
     9	  billing_error: 'auth',
    10	};
    11	
    12	// eslint-disable-next-line no-control-regex
    13	const ANSI = /\x1b\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z]/g;
    14	
    15	/** @param {any} matcher @param {string} text */
    16	function matches(matcher, text) {
    17	  switch (matcher.kind) {
    18	    case 'substring':
    19	      return text.includes(matcher.value);
    20	    case 'regex':
    21	      return new RegExp(matcher.pattern, matcher.flags || '').test(text);
    22	    case 'json-field': {
    23	      for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    24	        let obj;
    25	        try {
    26	          obj = JSON.parse(line);
    27	        } catch {
    28	          continue;
    29	        }
    30	        const val = String(matcher.path)
    31	          .split('.')
    32	          .reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
    33	        if (val === matcher.equals) return true;
    34	      }
    35	      return false;
    36	    }
    37	    default:
    38	      return false;
    39	  }
    40	}
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Classify harness output. Structured evidence first (text and exit code are
    44	 * ignored when a recognized structured signal is present); text signatures as
    45	 * the fallback tier; exit code only breaks the no-match tie — a generic nonzero
    46	 * exit is never usage-limit on its own.
    47	 * @param {{text?: string, exitCode?: number, platform: string, table: any, structured?: {kind: string, errorType: string} | null}} input
    48	 * @returns {{class: string, signatureId: string | null, confidence: string | null, resetHint: string | null, tried?: string[]}}
    49	 */
    50	export function classify({ text = '', exitCode = 0, platform, table, structured = null }) {
    51	  if (structured) {
    52	    if (structured.kind === 'stop-failure') {
    53	      const cls = STOP_FAILURE_MAP[structured.errorType] ?? 'other-error';
    54	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    55	    }
    56	    if (structured.kind === 'exec-json') {
    57	      const cls = structured.errorType === 'usage_limit' ? 'usage-limit' : 'other-error';
    58	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    59	    }
    60	    // Unrecognized structured kind: fall through to the text tier.
    61	  }
    62	
    63	  const stripped = text.replace(ANSI, '');
    64	  const candidates = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform);
    65	  /** @type {any} */
    66	  let best = null;
    67	  for (const s of candidates) {
    68	    if (!matches(s.matcher, stripped)) continue;
    69	    if (best === null || PRECEDENCE.indexOf(s.class) < PRECEDENCE.indexOf(best.class)) best = s;
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  if (best) {
    73	    let resetHint = null;
    74	    if (typeof best.resetHint === 'string') {
    75	      const m = stripped.match(new RegExp(best.resetHint));
    76	      if (m) resetHint = m[1] ?? m[0];
    77	    }
    78	    return { class: best.class, signatureId: best.id, confidence: best.confidence, resetHint };
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  return {
    82	    class: exitCode !== 0 ? 'other-error' : 'ok',
    83	    signatureId: null,
    84	    confidence: null,
    85	    resetHint: null,
    86	  };
    87	}

     6	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     7	// Gate-2 fix 8 (reviewer-a finding 11): Codex and Cursor SessionStart hooks
     8	// used to run FULL receive preparation — exit-1 noise with no bundle, a resume
     9	// prompt for the session's own open bundle, and the wrong output shape for
    10	// Cursor. `baton session-start` is the cheap gate the adapters call instead:
    11	// it inspects only pending-foreign state (sealed OR limit-hit, AND from
    12	// another platform), emits the harness-specific context shape, and ALWAYS
    13	// exits 0 — a session-start notice must never break a session.
    14	//
    15	// Pending matrix mirrors the claude-code hook contract (S3):
    16	//   positive:  foreign SEALED bundle                       -> context
    17	//   positive:  foreign OPEN + reasonClass usage-limit      -> context
    18	//   negative:  foreign OPEN non-limit                      -> nothing
    19	//   negative:  own-platform sealed / open                  -> nothing
    20	//   negative:  no bundle / no .handoff                     -> nothing
    21	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22	
    23	const T0 = '2026-07-12T00:00:00.000Z';
    24	
    25	function bundleWith(overrides = {}) {
    26	  const b = {
    27	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
    28	    bundleId: 'b_sessionstart00',
    29	    generation: 1,
    30	    createdAt: T0,
    31	    updatedAt: T0,
    32	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 'sess-cc', unstable: false },
    33	    task: { goal: 'g', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
    34	    plan: { steps: [] },
    35	    decisions: [],
    36	    files: { touched: [] },
    37	    roles: { assignments: {} },
    38	    git: null,
    39	    handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
    40	    journalSeq: 0,
    41	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
    42	    dedupeRing: [],
    43	  };
    44	  return { ...b, ...overrides, handoff: { ...b.handoff, ...(overrides.handoff ?? {}) }, origin: { ...b.origin, ...(overrides.origin ?? {}) } };
    45	}

     3	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
     4	import { cmdRecover } from '../../core/src/commands/recover.mjs';
     5	
     6	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     7	// Gate-2 fix 11 (reviewer-a finding 14): `recover` sat in the frozen command
     8	// list returning not-implemented. cmdRecover wraps recoverLock and keeps its
     9	// one hard rule: exactly ONE state recovers — a provably-dead same-host owner.
    10	// `--force` is an intent flag only; live, cross-host, and torn states refuse
    11	// with the same reasons, force or not.
    12	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13	
    14	const OWNER = (over = {}) => JSON.stringify({ host: 'host-A', pid: 999, startTime: 5, fencingToken: 't', acquiredAt: 'x', heartbeatAt: 'x', ...over });
    15	
    16	describe('cmdRecover — the one recoverable state', () => {
    17	  it('free lock: exit 0, nothing to do', async () => {
    18	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': '{}' } });
    19	    assert.equal(await cmdRecover([], io), 0);
    20	    assert.match(io.stdoutText(), /recovered|free|nothing/i);
    21	  });
    22	
    23	  it('provably-dead same-host owner: cleared, journal note recorded, exit 0', async () => {
    24	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/lock/owner.json': OWNER() } });
    25	    // default processAlive: only THIS io's pid/startTime is alive -> 999 is dead
    26	    assert.equal(await cmdRecover([], io), 0);
    27	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync('/repo/.handoff/lock'), false, 'stale lock removed');
    28	    assert.match(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson'] ?? '', /recovery/i);
    29	  });
    30	
    31	  it('live same-host owner: refused with exit 1, EVEN with --force', async () => {
    32	    const files = { '/repo/.handoff/lock/owner.json': OWNER() };
    33	    const alive = () => true;
    34	    const io = makeIo({ files, processAlive: alive });
    35	    assert.equal(await cmdRecover([], io), 1);
    36	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /live process/i);
    37	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync('/repo/.handoff/lock'), true, 'the live owner keeps its lock');
    38	
    39	    const io2 = makeIo({ files, processAlive: alive });
    40	    assert.equal(await cmdRecover(['--force'], io2), 1, 'force never overrides a live owner');
    41	    assert.equal(io2.fs.existsSync('/repo/.handoff/lock'), true);
    42	  });
    43	
    44	  it('cross-host owner: refused, exit 1', async () => {
    45	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/lock/owner.json': OWNER({ host: 'other-host' }) } });
    46	    assert.equal(await cmdRecover(['--force'], io), 1);
    47	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /another host/i);
    48	  });
    49	
    50	  it('torn metadata (lock dir without owner.json): refused, exit 1', async () => {
    51	    const io = makeIo({ files: {} });
    52	    io.fs.mkdirSync('/repo/.handoff/lock', { recursive: true });
    53	    assert.equal(await cmdRecover([], io), 1);
    54	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /torn|unsupported/i);
    55	  });

    62	    assert.equal(env.data.recovered, true);

    10	
    11	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12	// Gate-2 fix 11 (reviewer-b finding 9): the --json contract is "exactly one
    13	// compact envelope {ok,data,warnings,error} on stdout" for EVERY exit path —
    14	// checkpoint emitted none and usage errors bypassed the envelope everywhere.
    15	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16	
    17	/** stdout must be exactly one parseable envelope line. */
    18	function theEnvelope(io) {
    19	  const out = io.stdoutText().trim();
    20	  const lines = out === '' ? [] : out.split('\n');
    21	  assert.equal(lines.length, 1, `expected exactly one stdout line, got: ${JSON.stringify(io.stdoutText())}`);
    22	  const env = JSON.parse(lines[0]);
    23	  for (const k of ['ok', 'data', 'warnings', 'error']) assert.ok(k in env, `envelope carries ${k}`);
    24	  return env;
    25	}
    26	
    27	const EVENT = JSON.stringify({ schema: 'baton/event@1', type: 'decision', payload: { summary: 's' } });
    28

    31	    const io = makeIo({ stdin: EVENT });
    32	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code', '--json'], io), 0);
    33	    const env = theEnvelope(io);
    34	    assert.equal(env.ok, true);
    35	    assert.equal(typeof env.data.events, 'number');
    36	  });
    37	
    38	  it('usage error (no --platform): envelope with a usage error, exit 2', async () => {
    39	    const io = makeIo({ stdin: EVENT });

    41	    const env = theEnvelope(io);
    42	    assert.equal(env.ok, false);
    43	    assert.equal(env.error.code, 'usage');
    44	  });
    45	
    46	  it('garbage stdin: envelope ok:false, exit stays 0 (hook-safety)', async () => {

    49	    const env = theEnvelope(io);
    50	    assert.equal(env.ok, false);
    51	  });
    52	});

docs/plans/2026-07-11-baton-v1.md:35:  - Cursor: weakest — BYO-key returns `{"error":"Too Many Requests", …}`; managed-billing schema undocumented. Heuristics + timeouts, `confidence: low`.
docs/plans/2026-07-11-baton-v1.md:58:**Concurrency** (Gate-1 additions, iterations 1+2): all bundle mutations run under a repo-scoped lock — `.handoff/lock` acquired via atomic `mkdir`/`O_EXCL`, containing `{writerId: <platform>-<pid>-<sessionHint>, fencingToken: <random per acquisition>, acquiredAt, heartbeatAt}`. **Lock model (iterations 3+4)**: mutation locks are **operation-scoped** — acquired for the duration of one command's mutation and released, never held across turns; logical session ownership lives in `bundle.json` (the active `origin`/generation), not in the lock. Lock metadata (host, pid, process start time, fencing token) is written atomically with acquisition; a crash between lock creation and metadata publication reads as torn metadata. Recovery rules, conservative on every OS: automatic recovery only when the owner is **provably dead** (same host, pid gone or pid/start-time mismatch); a same-host owner confirmed alive is **refused** — including `recover --force` — with the instruction to terminate that process first; missing/torn/cross-host/unverifiable owner metadata is treated as **unsupported for automatic recovery** (refusal with explanation), never as safe-to-steal. Fencing tokens remain a fast-abort layer. Tests: force attempted against a live paused writer (refused), crash between lock creation and metadata publication, owner paused after its final token check, torn-metadata refusal. Journal entries carry `writerId`; sequence numbers are allocated under the lock. **Semantic isolation**: v1 enforces one active task — a checkpoint whose `sessionHint`/origin doesn't match the active bundle is rejected with an actionable warning (`--take-over` supersedes: archives the current bundle to `history/` and starts fresh), so two unrelated sessions can't merge goals/files/decisions into one handoff. `receive` is a core-owned two-phase transaction. `receive --prepare` validates + resolves roles + returns a one-time receipt token and the resume prompt without mutating state; the token **binds** the bundle revision (journalSeq), normalized intake, config digest, target platform+session, the probe snapshot used for remap, **and a content-sensitive prepare-time git digest** — HEAD + branch + hashes of the staged and unstaged diffs + a bounded untracked-files digest (path + size + content hash, capped) — so a file that stays `M` but changes content still invalidates the token. `--commit` re-derives and compares every bound input, rejecting any drift (intervening checkpoint, config edit, HEAD move, content change, probe change) and requiring re-prepare; each declared drift input has its own rejection test. `receive --commit <token>` performs one atomic transition: `sealed → received` (normal) or `open → received` (the primary failure case: limit death before finalize; commit writes a receive-generated degraded seal recording intake origin/reason, flagged `degraded-seal`) — and in the same transition **adopts the receiving platform/session as the bundle's active owner and opens a fresh writable generation** (`generation` counter incremented, `status: open`), archiving the prior seal + receipt to `history/`. The receiving session's subsequent checkpoints are therefore first-party, and the bundle is immediately receivable again if the new platform also dies — chained A→B→C failover is contract-tested with checkpoints fired immediately after each receive. Cancellation or a competing receiver leaves state untouched; double-commit fails cleanly. Identical semantics on every adapter; six-direction contract tests cover sealed + unsealed variants. Tests: concurrent writers, provably-dead takeover, fencing-token abort incl. pause-after-final-check, foreign-session rejection/take-over, prepare-vs-checkpoint races, prepare-without-commit, double-commit, competing receivers, chained failovers.
docs/plans/2026-07-11-baton-v1.md:68:**Limit detector**: two tiers — **structured signals first** (Claude Code `StopFailure` matcher error types; documented limit-specific fields in codex JSON output), text signatures as fallback. **Exit-code rule (iteration 3): a generic nonzero exit never classifies as usage-limit on its own** — exit 10 requires limit-specific structured or text evidence; nonzero without evidence = `other-error`. Fixture corpus includes non-limit failing processes. Signatures are **versioned data** (`core/data/signatures.v1.json`), seeded with the verified strings for all three platforms, matcher kinds `substring`/`regex`/`json-field`, per-signature confidence, precedence usage-limit > auth > throttle > other-error, `--signatures <path>` overlay for hot-patching drift, `--explain` for auditability. Hardening (Gate-1 additions): input is ANSI-stripped before matching; the fixture corpus includes negative/near-miss cases (throttle-vs-usage confusables, multiline, malformed JSON, localized text) so false positives are regression-tested; overlay regexes are lint-checked (length cap, no backreferences, linear-time constructs) and matching carries a time guard; a low-confidence match **never silently avoids a platform** — it warns and defers to user intake at receive. Cursor entries carry `confidence: low|medium` explicitly.
docs/plans/2026-07-11-baton-v1.md:72:**Receive**: loads bundle with recovery + staleness checks (age > 12 h; HEAD moved; dirty mismatch), merges intake flags (`--origin`, `--reason`), resolves roles, and runs as the **prepare/commit transaction defined under Concurrency** — prompt emission at prepare, state mutation only at commit.      1	const TRUNC_SUFFIX = '…[truncated]';
     2	
     3	export const DEFAULT_BUDGET = {
     4	  decisionsMax: 200,
     5	  keepFirst: 20,
     6	  keepLast: 150,
     7	  detailMax: 2000,
     8	  filesMax: 300,
     9	  dirtyMax: 100,
    10	};
    11	
    12	/**
    13	 * Deterministic size-budget enforcement. Pure: returns a new bundle. Truncation
    14	 * only — never summarization — so the output is reproducible byte-for-byte and
    15	 * the full history stays in journal.ndjson.
    16	 * @param {any} bundle
    17	 * @param {Partial<typeof DEFAULT_BUDGET>} [budget]
    18	 */
    19	export function compact(bundle, budget = {}) {
    20	  const B = { ...DEFAULT_BUDGET, ...budget };
    21	  const out = structuredClone(bundle);
    22	
    23	  if (out.decisions.length > B.decisionsMax) {
    24	    const dropped = out.decisions.length - (B.keepFirst + B.keepLast);
    25	    const marker = {
    26	      summary: `[compacted: ${dropped} earlier decisions omitted — full log in journal.ndjson]`,
    27	    };
    28	    out.decisions = [
    29	      ...out.decisions.slice(0, B.keepFirst),
    30	      marker,
    31	      ...out.decisions.slice(out.decisions.length - B.keepLast),
    32	    ];
    33	    out.compaction.droppedDecisions += dropped;
    34	  }
    35	
    36	  for (const d of out.decisions) {
    37	    if (typeof d.detail === 'string' && d.detail.length > B.detailMax) {
    38	      d.detail = d.detail.slice(0, B.detailMax - TRUNC_SUFFIX.length) + TRUNC_SUFFIX;
    39	    }
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  if (out.files.touched.length > B.filesMax) {
    43	    out.files.touched = [...out.files.touched]
    44	      .sort((a, b) => (a.lastTs < b.lastTs ? 1 : a.lastTs > b.lastTs ? -1 : 0))
    45	      .slice(0, B.filesMax);
    46	    out.files.truncated = true;
    47	  }
    48	
    49	  if (out.git && Array.isArray(out.git.dirtySummary) && out.git.dirtySummary.length > B.dirtyMax) {
    50	    out.git.dirtySummary = out.git.dirtySummary.slice(0, B.dirtyMax);
    51	    out.git.summaryTruncated = true;
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  return out;
    55	}

codex
Several disposition tests are asserting the presence of a marker or a warning rather than the promised recovered state or atomic behavior. I’ve found concrete mismatches in journal-only recovery, low-confidence remapping, and the adapter commit command; I’m reproducing the pure/memory-backed cases now before fixing the final severity and closure count.

     1	import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
     2	
     3	// Git facts degrade to null rather than block a checkpoint: not-a-repo,
     4	// missing binary, and timeouts are all expected environments (plan §Root
     5	// discovery & degraded modes).
     6	const GIT_TIMEOUT_MS = 5000;
     7	// The untracked digest is bounded: beyond this many paths, entries fold in by
     8	// name only, keeping prepare-time hashing cheap on huge untracked trees.
     9	const UNTRACKED_CAP = 200;
    10	// NUL separator: cannot occur in diffs, sizes, hex hashes, or file paths, so
    11	// joined digest inputs cannot collide across field boundaries.
    12	const SEP = String.fromCharCode(0);
    13	const NEWLINE = String.fromCharCode(10);
    14	
    15	const sha256 = (/** @type {string} */ s) => createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * Capture the repo's git state plus a content-sensitive digest. The digest
    19	 * covers the staged diff, unstaged diff, and a bounded untracked-files digest
    20	 * (path + size + content hash) — NOT the HEAD sha, which the receive token
    21	 * binds as its own input, so a HEAD-only move is distinguishable from a
    22	 * content change.
    23	 * @param {{execFile: Function, cwd: string, fs: any}} deps
    24	 * @returns {Promise<{branch: string, headSha: string, dirty: boolean, dirtySummary: string[], contentDigest: string} | null>}
    25	 */
    26	export async function snapshot({ execFile, cwd, fs }) {
    27	  const git = (/** @type {string[]} */ args) => execFile('git', args, { cwd, timeout: GIT_TIMEOUT_MS });
    28	  let branch, head, status, staged, unstaged, untrackedList;
    29	  try {
    30	    [branch, head, status, staged, unstaged, untrackedList] = await Promise.all([
    31	      git(['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD']),
    32	      git(['rev-parse', 'HEAD']),
    33	      git(['status', '--porcelain']),
    34	      git(['diff', '--cached']),
    35	      git(['diff']),
    36	      git(['ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard']),
    37	    ]);
    38	  } catch {
    39	    return null;
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  const nonEmptyLines = (/** @type {string} */ s) => s.split(NEWLINE).filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l !== '');
    43	  const dirtySummary = nonEmptyLines(status.stdout);
    44	  const untrackedPaths = nonEmptyLines(untrackedList.stdout);
    45	
    46	  const untrackedEntries = untrackedPaths.map((rel, i) => {
    47	    if (i >= UNTRACKED_CAP) return [rel, 'capped'].join(SEP);
    48	    try {
    49	      const content = fs.readFileSync(cwd + '/' + rel, 'utf8');
    50	      return [rel, String(Buffer.byteLength(content)), sha256(content)].join(SEP);
    51	    } catch {
    52	      // Listed but unreadable (deleted mid-flight): still digest its presence.
    53	      return [rel, 'absent'].join(SEP);
    54	    }
    55	  });
    56	
    57	  return {
    58	    branch: branch.stdout.trim(),
    59	    headSha: head.stdout.trim(),
    60	    dirty: dirtySummary.length > 0,
    61	    dirtySummary,
    62	    contentDigest: sha256([staged.stdout, unstaged.stdout, ...untrackedEntries].join(SEP)),
    63	  };
    64	}

     1	import { loadConfig } from '../roles/matrix.mjs';
     2	import { resolveRoles } from '../roles/resolve.mjs';
     3	import { readProbeCache } from '../roles/availability.mjs';
     4	import { emitEnvelope, parseFlags, resolveRoot, usageError } from './shared.mjs';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * `baton remap` — resolve the committed role matrix for a destination platform.
     8	 * Read-only: prints assignments, never mutates the bundle (receive owns that).
     9	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    10	 * @returns {number}
    11	 */
    12	export function cmdRemap(args, io) {
    13	  const { flags } = parseFlags(args);
    14	  const to = typeof flags.to === 'string' ? flags.to : null;
    15	  if (!to) return usageError(io, flags, 'remap', '--to <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    16	
    17	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    18	  const { config, errors } = loadConfig(root, io);
    19	  if (!config) {
    20	    const msg = errors.map((e) => (e.path ? `${e.path}: ${e.msg}` : e.msg)).join('; ');
    21	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-config', msg } });
    22	    else io.stderr.write(`baton remap: ${msg}\n`);
    23	    return 1;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  const avoid = typeof flags.avoid === 'string' ? flags.avoid.split(',') : [];
    27	  const { assignments, notes } = resolveRoles({
    28	    config,
    29	    to,
    30	    avoid,
    31	    nativeOnly: flags['native-only'] === true,
    32	    // The fresh doctor probe cache feeds eligibility (gate-2 major 13):
    33	    // rate-limited platforms are skipped like avoid[] entries; a stale or
    34	    // absent cache resolves offline with degraded flags.
    35	    probes: readProbeCache(root, io),
    36	  });
    37	
    38	  if (flags.json) {
    39	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { assignments, notes } });
    40	  } else {
    41	    for (const [role, a] of Object.entries(assignments)) {
    42	      io.stdout.write(`${role}: ${a.platform ?? '—'}/${a.model ?? '—'}${a.effort ? `@${a.effort}` : ''} (${a.mode})\n`);
    43	    }
    44	  }
    45	  return 0;
    46	}
     1	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     2	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     3	import { bundlePaths, loadBundle, writeSnapshot, rotateJournal } from '../bundle/store.mjs';
     4	import { snapshot as gitSnapshot } from '../git/snapshot.mjs';
     5	import { loadSignatures } from '../detect/signatures.mjs';
     6	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
     7	import { emitEnvelope, parseFlags, resolveRoot, usageError } from './shared.mjs';
     8	
     9	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * Infer the reason class by running the reason text through the classifier
    13	 * against the bundle's origin platform. No signature match reads as 'ok',
    14	 * which is not a switch reason — degrade to null rather than mislabel.
    15	 * @param {string} reason @param {string} platform @param {any} io
    16	 * @returns {string | null}
    17	 */
    18	function inferReasonClass(reason, platform, io) {
    19	  try {
    20	    const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
    21	    const verdict = classify({ text: reason, exitCode: 0, platform, table, structured: null });
    22	    return verdict.class === 'ok' ? null : verdict.class;
    23	  } catch {
    24	    return null;
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	/**
    29	 * `baton finalize` — the narrative seal: records the switch reason, refreshes
    30	 * git state, seals the bundle, and freezes this generation into history via a
    31	 * 'finalize' journal rotation.
    32	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    33	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    34	 */
    35	export async function cmdFinalize(args, io) {
    36	  const { flags } = parseFlags(args);
    37	  const reason = typeof flags.reason === 'string' ? flags.reason : null;
    38	  if (!reason) return usageError(io, flags, 'finalize', '--reason "<why you are switching>" is required');
    39	  const REASON_CLASSES = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
    40	  if (flags['reason-class'] !== undefined && !REASON_CLASSES.includes(/** @type {string} */ (flags['reason-class']))) {
    41	    return usageError(io, flags, 'finalize', `--reason-class must be one of ${REASON_CLASSES.join('|')}`);
    42	  }
    43	
    44	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    45	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
    46	  const { bundle, warnings } = loadBundle(root, io);
    47	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton finalize: ${w}\n`);
    48	  if (bundle === null) {
    49	    const error = { code: 'no-bundle', msg: `no handoff bundle at ${paths.snapshot}; nothing to finalize` };
    50	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error });
    51	    else io.stderr.write(`baton finalize: ${error.msg}\n`);
    52	    return 1;
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
    56	  if (git === null) io.stderr.write('baton finalize: git state unavailable — sealing without a git refresh\n');
    57	
    58	  const reasonClass =
    59	    typeof flags['reason-class'] === 'string' ? flags['reason-class'] : inferReasonClass(reason, bundle.origin.platform, io);
    60	
    61	  const sealed = {
    62	    ...bundle,
    63	    git: git ?? bundle.git,
    64	    updatedAt: io.now(),
    65	    handoff: {
    66	      ...bundle.handoff,
    67	      status: 'sealed',
    68	      reason,
    69	      reasonClass,
    70	      toPlatformHint: typeof flags.to === 'string' ? flags.to : null,
    71	      finalizedAt: io.now(),
    72	    },
    73	  };
    74	
    75	  writeSnapshot(root, sealed, io);
    76	  rotateJournal(root, 'finalize', io);
    77	
    78	  if (flags.json) {
    79	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { bundlePath: paths.snapshot, handoffMdPath: paths.handoffMd } });
    80	  } else {
    81	    io.stdout.write(`sealed — reason: ${reason}${reasonClass ? ` (${reasonClass})` : ''}${sealed.handoff.toPlatformHint ? ` — next: ${sealed.handoff.toPlatformHint}` : ''}\n`);
    82	  }
    83	  return 0;
    84	}

codex
Gate 2 remains blocked. The 131 targeted tests passed and `npm run typecheck` was clean, but direct memory-backed probes confirmed several tests overstate their assertions—for example, journal-only recovery still loses origin, malformed role assignments still crash rendering, and `baton --json` emits no envelope. Fix-range attribution is clean.

VERDICT: BLOCKED
DISPOSITIONS: 5 of 16 iteration-1 findings verified closed; NOT closed: 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 12, 14, 15, 16
FINDINGS:
1. [blocking] — core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs:239-253 — Git is refreshed only when an event is non-routine; ordinary Stop and file-touch mechanical checkpoints therefore retain stale/null git state even when their snapshot is rewritten — iteration-1 finding #4; plan §Checkpoint vocabulary and §Checkpoint engine — Fix: capture and persist bounded git state for every mechanical checkpoint, with an explicit unavailable warning/state.
2. [major] — core/src/commands/shared.mjs:35-45; core/src/util/jail.mjs:49-58 — Root ascent splits only on `/`, while the jail compares a native `realpathSync()` result against a forward-slash string; Windows paths consequently fail discovery and existing `.handoff` trees are rejected — iteration-1 findings #5/#6; plan §Root discovery and Windows + packaging task — Fix: use `node:path`/`path.win32` operations and volume-aware relative containment, with Windows-path tests.
3. [blocking] — core/src/bundle/lock.mjs:119-152 — Provably-dead takeover overwrites `owner.json` inside the already-existing lock directory without atomically claiming or recreating the lock; two contenders that both inspect the stale owner can both publish tokens and enter acquisition bookkeeping — plan §Concurrency atomic lock acquisition — Fix: atomically claim/remove-and-recreate the stale lock before publishing ownership, and barrier-test multiple contenders against one pre-created dead lock.
4. [blocking] — core/src/bundle/lock.mjs:110-115,245-257; adapters/claude-code/scripts/hook.mjs:48-75 — Lock acquisition/recovery and hook-error logging operate inside `.handoff` before any managed-tree jail check; `recoverLock` can remove an outside `lock` through a symlink and hook logging can write outside the repository — iteration-1 finding #6; plan §Threat model symlink refusal and diagnostics logging contract — Fix: jail before every lock/recovery/log mutation and add real-filesystem symlink tests for recover and the failing-hook path.
5. [blocking] — core/src/detect/signatures.mjs:88-104,127-145; core/src/detect/probe-worker.mjs:10-15 — Regex syntax is validated without matcher flags, while the worker swallows flag compilation errors as successful completion; an overlay with `flags:"z"` loads and later throws in the classifier — iteration-1 finding #7; plan §Limit detector hardening — Fix: validate the exact pattern-plus-flags pair, reject unsupported/duplicate flags, and propagate worker compilation failures distinctly from timeouts.
6. [blocking] — core/src/bundle/schema.mjs:116-120; core/src/bundle/merge.mjs:71-74; core/src/bundle/store.mjs:147-149 — Nested validation remains incomplete: `{roles:{assignments:{planner:null}}}` validates and then crashes `renderHandoffMd`, and a parseable `null` journal line crashes replay before `applyEvent` — iteration-1 finding #8; plan bundle schema and §Threat model untrusted inputs — Fix: validate every consumed nested field and assignment value, validate journal event envelopes before replay, and degrade malformed entries to audit warnings.
7. [major] — core/src/commands/doctor.mjs:107-154; core/src/roles/resolve.mjs:20-29 — A successful `claude --version` is still recorded as authenticated/reachable, absent probe data is not marked degraded, and doctor does not report installed/enabled/trusted/observed as distinct states — iteration-1 finding #9; plan §Role matrix availability probing and §Codex/Cursor trust gates — Fix: separate version/install checks from authentication/reachability probes, represent unknown dimensions explicitly, mark offline selections degraded, and report each hook state separately.
8. [major] — core/src/receive/txn.mjs:21-25,110-114 — `classifyReason` discards confidence, so Cursor’s low-confidence “quota exceeded” heuristic silently adds Cursor to `avoid[]`; the reproduced prepare result skipped Cursor without a confidence warning — iteration-1 finding #9; plan §Limit detector low-confidence behavior — Fix: retain the full verdict, warn on low confidence, and require explicit intake confirmation/reason-class before avoidance.
9. [blocking] — adapters/claude-code/commands/receive.md:8-10 — The prepare command uses `${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}`, but the required commit step tells the harness to run bare `receive --platform …`, which is not an executable outside the baton repository — iteration-1 finding #10; plan §Claude Code adapter command surfaces — Fix: spell out the full safely quoted `node "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/core/bin/baton.mjs" receive …` commit command.
10. [major] — core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs:135-145 — Debounce checks and writes its stamp outside the repo lock, so simultaneous `afterFileEdit` hooks can both observe no stamp and both checkpoint — iteration-1 finding #12; plan §Cursor adapter afterFileEdit debounce — Fix: serialize the stamp check-and-set under the repo lock and add a barrier-controlled concurrent debounce test.
11. [major] — core/src/cli.mjs:64-87; core/src/commands/receive.mjs:63-65 — JSON output was only partially centralized: `baton --json` produces no stdout envelope, and `receive --print-prompt --json` emits raw Markdown — iteration-1 finding #14; plan §CLI surface and docs/design/core.md §CLI contract — Fix: parse global JSON intent before command dispatch/help and route every success, usage, and failure path through one envelope emitter.
12. [major] — core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs:192-203; core/src/bundle/merge.mjs:33-41; core/src/bundle/store.mjs:101-108 — The new seed is merely a note: replay ignores `payload.seed`, so deleting snapshot and backup rebuilds origin as `unknown` and loses bundle identity/task metadata; the test only checks that the decision text mentions the platform — iteration-1 finding #15; plan §Bundle recovery paths — Fix: introduce a self-applicable seed/state event carrying bundle identity, origin, task and generation, and assert those fields after journal-only recovery.
13. [blocking] — tests/integration/e2e-failover.test.mjs:646-660; tests/integration/lock-concurrency.test.mjs:112-184 — The new barrier tests cover writers and lock holders, but competing receiver commits are still invoked sequentially, so the required genuinely concurrent receiver acceptance case remains absent — iteration-1 finding #16; plan Acceptance constraint 6 and §Concurrency tests — Fix: prepare two receipts, release two commit child processes from one barrier, and assert exactly one complete transition/archive with the loser leaving state untouched.
